# 🟢 A1.1 — Existence

**Mode:** `AX_CORE` | **Atlas Type:** `Axiom` | **Status:** `primitive`
**Domain:** Primordial | **Legacy ID:** `AX-001`

---

## Statement

Something exists rather than nothing.

## Formal Expression

```
∃x (existential quantifier — irreducible)
```

## Maximum Defensible Position

At least one thing exists; equivalently, the chosen domain of discourse is nonempty.

## Classification

|-------|-------|
| **Mode** | `AX_CORE` |
| **Atlas Object Type** | `Axiom` |
| **Workbook Role** | `AXIOM` |

| **Domain** | Primordial |

## Dependency Chain

**Anchor:** A1.1 (Existence) — ROOT
**Depends On:** ∅ (foundational)
**Chain Tier:** 0 — Primordial Foundation

## References

Leibniz 1714; Heidegger 1927

## Defeat Conditions

Show absolute nothingness is coherent and stable.

## Limitations (from workbook)

Strongest axiom. Near-universally accepted. Only challenge: radical Buddhist śūnyatā reading.

# 🟢 A1.2 — Distinction

**Mode:** `AX_CORE` | **Atlas Type:** `Axiom` | **Status:** `primitive`
**Domain:** Primordial | **Legacy ID:** `AX-002`

---

## Statement

For anything to be describable or knowable, it must be distinguishable from something else.

## Formal Expression

```
∀x∀y(x≠y → Distinguishable(x,y))
```

## Maximum Defensible Position

The framework presently assumes or requires: For anything to be describable or knowable, it must be distinguishable from something else.

## Classification

| **Mode** | `AX_CORE` |
| **Atlas Object Type** | `Axiom` |

---

```

```

Information is defined as resolved uncertainty — Shannon entropy.

I(x) = log₂(1/p(x)); H(X) = -Σp·log p
```

## Maximum Defensible Position

Definition: Information = resolved uncertainty.

**Mode:** `AX_SCAFFOLD` | **Atlas Type:** `Definition` | **Status:** `definition`
**Domain:** Ontological | **Legacy ID:** `AX-011`

---

## Statement

Properties of the χ-field: scalar, real-valued, non-negative, Lorentz-invariant.

## Formal Expression

```
χ: scalar, real-valued, non-negative, Lorentz-invariant
```

## Maximum Defensible Position

Definition: χ-field properties specified.

## Classification

**Mode:** `AX_SCAFFOLD` | **Atlas Type:** `Definition` | **Status:** `definition`
**Domain:** Ontological | **Legacy ID:** `AX-018`

---

## Statement

Coherence functional defined as normalized gradient measure of χ.

## Formal Expression

```
C[χ] = ∫|∇χ|²dV / ∫|χ|²dV — gradient coherence functional
```

## Maximum Defensible Position

Definition: C[χ] = coherence functional.

Show coherence is better measured differently. Demonstrate this functional misses key coherence features.

**Domain:** Ontological | **Legacy ID:** `AX-029`

---

Kolmogorov complexity defined — algorithmic information content.

K(x) = min{|p| : U(p) = x} — shortest program producing x
```

Definition: K(x) = Kolmogorov complexity.
